- **sp-loc-papers**: Use when a claim rests on a letter, diary, speech draft, telegram, scrapbook or other manuscript in the Library of Congress Theodore Roosevelt Papers, or in related Library of Congress family papers (Kermit and Belle Roosevelt, Archibald Roosevelt, Theodore Roosevelt Jr., Alice Roosevelt Longworth). Checks that the document exists, who wrote it to whom, its date, and what it actually says. Any first-to-use-X claim also goes to sp-superlatives. Use for any diagnosis, injury, treatment, cause of death, or medical anecdote. Military wound facts for his sons go to sp-family-offices.
